Article: (CNN)An ancient ring has shed new light on the ties between Vikings and the Islamic world, more than a century after its discovery. The finger ring found in a 9th century Swedish grave is inscribed with Arabic Kufic writing. In a paper published in the journal Scanning, the researchers say it is the only ring with an Arabic inscription ever found at a Scandinavian archaeological site. The young American is hoping to become the first woman to win both the discipline's world title and overall crown in successive seasons. Shiffrin, who retained her slalom title at February's world championships in Colorado, now has a 90-point lead going into next week's World Cup finale in France. I learned that I have to keep really calm and really focused and really relaxed in the start. And that way, whatever happens, happens."

 (See on top of this post) The theory "Text Simplification" could help you understand this text much better. wrote: The theory "Text Simplification" could help you understand this text much better. This is a chemical element. The chemical symbol for gold is Au, whose name stems from the Latin word aurum meaning "shining dawn", and was adopted by the Spanish and Portuguese during their colonial times in the Americas from the American native word for gold. Gold, unlike silver and platinum, is not a reactive metal and does not react with oxygen or water at room temperature. Even when heated, gold does not burn like most metals. Gold has two crystalline forms. At normal temperatures and pressures, it is always found in the more stable cubic form called face - centered cubic, in which each gold atom is surrounded by twelve others in a regular pattern known as a closest packed structure. Above 30°C, a relatively large number of atoms adopts a denser hexagonal close-packed structure. somewhere between these forms and most samples of gold, particularly those obtained by melting, consist of around 60% cubic and 40% hexagonal structures. Gold has some compounds that are colored, such as the chloride CaAuCl, or the oxide Au2O3.

(Ad) Economies of Scale and the Supremacy of Large Scale Units. In every economy, agribusinesses are engaged in a constant struggle against the small farmer. This is especially true of the industrialized economies. The large-sized units have many advantages in the areas of transportation, machinery, storage capacity and sources of additional inputs, which are normally not available to smaller units. This has led to the disadvantaged positions of smaller agricultural units in financial terms and makes them economically struggling units and economic victims. The economies of scale gained through size and investment lead to market domination and a decision power which is secured by a centralized control over market places, land utilization and in activities like farming, transportation and promotion. Chalachew Seyoum, a graduate student from Ethiopia, came across the fossil -- the left side of a lower jaw with five teeth -- while on a field expedition in the Afar region of eastern Ethiopia in 2013.
